TC_OUTPUT_DELAY Actived set_output_delay -clock pi_sclk -max 3.000 [get_ports {pi_miso}]
TC_OUTPUT_DELAY Actived set_output_delay -clock pi_sclk -min -0.500 [get_ports {pi_miso}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_ports {i_resetn}]
TC_CLOCK_GROUP Actived set_clock_groups -asynchronous -group {sys_clk} -group {pi_sclk}
